import {
  defaultRailPrefs,
  resolveRailGroups,
  type RailItem,
  type RailPrefs,
} from "@/lib/railCatalog";

// The sidebar layout editor. Changes save as they are made — rail order is a
// low-stakes preference, so there is no draft/Save/Discard state to lose.

export default function RailLayoutEditor() {
  const { prefs, save, saving, loading, error, retry, canSave, ownerId } = useRailPrefs();
  const permissions = useUserPermissions();
  const [justSaved, setJustSaved] = React.useState(false);
  const savedTimer = React.useRef<number | null>(null);
  const ownerRef = React.useRef(ownerId);
  ownerRef.current = ownerId;

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setJustSaved(false);
    if (savedTimer.current) window.clearTimeout(savedTimer.current);
  }, [ownerId]);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (savedTimer.current) window.clearTimeout(savedTimer.current);
    };
  }, []);

  const commit = async (next: RailPrefs) => {
    const savingOwner = ownerId;
    setJustSaved(false);
    try {
      await save(next);
      if (ownerRef.current !== savingOwner) return;
      setJustSaved(true);
      if (savedTimer.current) window.clearTimeout(savedTimer.current);
      savedTimer.current = window.setTimeout(() => setJustSaved(false), 1600);
    } catch {
      if (ownerRef.current !== savingOwner) return;
      toast.error("Could not save your layout. Please try again.");
    }
  };

Yes, it can go in the Teacher Workspace. The catch is that I can't find a "Tools" category anywhere in the code. I don't want to guess at it and then build in the wrong place.

**Where I looked**
- `feat/reading-center` and origin/main (main has no new commits).
- Every local and remote branch.
- The shared `lsatu` checkout, including its uncommitted files.
- The sidebar layout editor and its saved categories.

**What exists today**
- **Left sidebar, "Teaching" group** (only you see it): Teacher Workspace, Students, Whiteboard.
- **Room tabs inside Teacher Workspace:** Today, Inbox, Payments, Students, Library, Teaching Assistant, Insights. Whiteboard and Assistant are buttons in the header.
- **"Tools" heading on Today:** a small label over the Today modes list (`src/pages/teacher/desk/ModeList.tsx:33`). It's a list of task modes, not a menu where a new page could go.

So either the Tools category hasn't reached GitHub yet (for example, it lives only in a Lovable session or another machine's checkout), or you mean one of the places above. Can you send a screenshot of where you see it?

If it really doesn't exist yet, here's what I'd do: add a **Tools** room to the Teacher Workspace tabs, put Reading Center inside it, and serve the pages under `/teacher/tools/reading-center`. That replaces the URL-only `/reading-center` routes in the plan, and it gives future tools a home.

Should I take "just make it" as your go to start the build? That's Step 10, then 11–17, with the Tools placement worked into Step 16. The migration still waits for a separate yes right before I apply it.

# Reading Center build: lessons from review

Gemini reads this file **before starting every chunk**. Claude appends a lesson after each review that
found a mistake. Each lesson is a rule to follow from now on. Newest at the bottom. Never delete lines.

1. Never stash, restore or switch branches in the shared checkout `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os-projects\lsatu`. Work only in `lsatu-reading-center`.
2. When a plan-supplied test fails, stop and hand off with the exact failure (as you did in chunk B). Do not edit the test or the code to force a pass. The plan can be wrong; the supervisor decides.
3. Before handing off, run `git status --short`; it must be empty. Commit any work in progress with a `WIP:` prefix rather than leaving it uncommitted.
4. Reading font, size and line spacing apply ONLY to passage text. `ReaderSurface` exposes them as CSS variables; put `style={READING_TEXT_STYLE}` (exported from `components/ReaderSurface.tsx`) on the element that holds the passage words. Never on bars, buttons or dialogs.
---

## Rules you must follow the whole time

1. **Branch, never `main`.** Step 0 creates the branch. Never run `git push origin main`, never
   `git reset --hard`, never `git push --force`.
2. **Touch only the files this plan names.** The one exception: a test file next to a file you created.
   Do not reformat, rename or "clean up" any other file.
3. **Do not edit** `src/integrations/supabase/types.ts`, `src/integrations/supabase/client.ts`,
   `CLAUDE.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `GEMINI.md`, or any existing migration file.
4. **Do not apply database migrations.** Write the `.sql` file only. Do not run `supabase db push`,
   do not call any Lovable tool, do not send Lovable any message. The reviewer applies the migration.
5. **No new npm packages.** Everything needed is already installed (React, react-router-dom,
   @tanstack/react-query, recharts, shadcn/ui in `src/components/ui/`, lucide-react, vitest).
6. **No em dashes (—) in any text a user sees.** Use a comma, a colon or a period instead.
   (The seed passage in step 2 is the one exception: it is source text, copy it exactly.)
7. **Because the database types are not regenerated yet**, every Supabase call to a `reading_center_*`
   table must go through the helper `rcDb()` from step 4. Never write `supabase.from("reading_center_...")`
   anywhere else.
8. **Imports** use the `@/` alias, e.g. `import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";`.
9. TypeScript strict mode is off in this repo. Still give every exported function explicit parameter and
   return types.
10. Commit after every step with the exact message given. Run `npm run typecheck` before every commit;
    if it fails, fix only errors in files you created.

---

## Supervisor: ask Claude, headless

A Claude supervisor oversees this build. It is the same Claude chat that wrote this plan, so it already
knows everything. You talk to it by running the `claude` command in your terminal. The command must run
**from `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os`** (that is where the chat lives), so it always starts with `cd`.

Use this exact command every time, changing only the message in the last quotes:

```bash
cd C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os && claude -p --resume 70b72262-5c64-4c94-b8d5-90f16a760c0b --add-dir C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os-projects\lsatu-reading-center "[Gemini] <your message>"
```

Always start your message with `[Gemini]` and include the step number, what you did, and the exact
question or the exact error (last 40 lines). Your first message, right after Step 0, must be:
`[Gemini] Starting. Step 0 done, worktree at C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os-projects\lsatu-reading-center on feat/reading-center. Ready for supervision.`

If the command errors (for example the session is busy), wait 60 seconds and retry once. If it fails
again, stop and tell Joshua.

When you **must** call it:
1. **Before you ask Joshua any question.** Ask the supervisor first. Only ask Joshua if the supervisor
   says "ask Joshua".
2. **When a Check fails twice.** Send the step number, the command, and the last 40 lines of output.
3. **After Steps 5, 10, 12 and 16** (checkpoint reviews). Message:
   `[Gemini] Checkpoint: Step N done. Review the diff for this step against the plan. Reply APPROVED or list exact fixes.`
   If it lists fixes, make exactly those fixes, commit, and send the checkpoint message again until it
   replies APPROVED.
4. **Before Step 19** (final review). Message: `[Gemini] Final review before PR. Review the full diff.`

## Step 10: The five mode renderers (`components/modes/`)

Every mode component receives the same props:

```ts
export interface ModeProps {
  tokens: Token[];
  chunks: Chunk[];
  chunk: number;              // current chunk index
  prefs: Preferences;
  playing: boolean;
  onWordClick: (wordIndex: number) => void; // used for jump and bookmarks
}
```

Put `ModeProps` in `components/modes/types.ts`.

**Font rule (applies to every mode, the View panel body and the Sample Text preview):** the passage text
element must use `style={READING_TEXT_STYLE}` (import from `../ReaderSurface`). Never set the reading font
size anywhere else, and never apply it to the top bar, bottom bar, buttons or dialogs. For the Sample Text
preview in the preferences dialog, set the inline style from the chosen font stack and size directly.

Then create:

### 10a. `SerialMode.tsx`
- Show only `chunks[chunk].rows`. Each row is one line: the words joined by a space.
- Container: flex column, vertically centred in the stage (`flex-1 flex items-center`).
- If `prefs.center_text` then `text-center justify-center items-center`, else left-aligned with
  `pl-[15%]`.
- When `playing` is false, each word is a `<button>` (no visible button styling, `cursor-pointer`)
  that calls `onWordClick(token.index)`.

### 10b. `FlowMode.tsx`
- Build "lines": group all tokens into rows of `prefs.words_per_line` tokens, never crossing a
  paragraph (reuse `chunkTokens(tokens, prefs.words_per_line, 1)`; each chunk is one line).
- Show only the current line's words at the position they would sit: render the **whole current line**
  but give every word outside the current chunk `opacity-0` (invisible, still takes space). The current
  chunk's words are fully visible. Left-aligned with `pl-[15%]`.
- Clickable words when paused, same as Serial.

### 10c. `HighlightMode.tsx`
- Render the whole passage. Group tokens into rows of `prefs.words_per_line` words (same grouping as
  Flow). Each row is a block line.
- Words with `index` between `chunks[chunk].startWord` and `chunks[chunk].endWord` (inclusive) have
  full opacity and `font-semibold`; every other word has `opacity-35` (use `style={{ opacity: 0.35 }}`).
- Paragraphs are separated by a gap (`mb-6` on the last row of each paragraph).
- Keep the current chunk vertically centred: give the first word of the current chunk a ref and, when
  `chunk` changes, call `ref.scrollIntoView({ block: "center", behavior: prefs.wpm > 600 ? "auto" : "smooth" })`.
- All words clickable at all times when paused.

### 10d. `ScrollMode.tsx`
- Same row layout as Highlight, inside a container with `overflow-hidden` and a fixed height (`h-[70vh]`).
- Draw a thin horizontal guide line at 40% of the container height (absolute positioned,
  `border-t border-current opacity-30`).
- Translate the inner column with CSS `transform: translateY(...)` so the row containing the current
  chunk's first word sits on the guide line. Use `transition: transform 150ms linear`. Measure the row's
  `offsetTop` with a ref on the row element.
- The current chunk's words are full opacity; others `0.6`.
- Clickable words when paused.

### 10e. `EReaderMode.tsx`
- No timer. Props additionally: `page: number`, `onPageCountChange(n)`, `onPageChange(n)`,
  `searchQuery: string`.
- Render paragraphs as normal `<p>` elements inside a column `max-w-[46rem] mx-auto px-6`.
- Pagination: put the text in a container with fixed height `calc(100vh - 140px)` and CSS columns:
  `columnWidth` = container width, `columnGap: 48px`, `height` fixed. Measure `scrollWidth / (width + 48)`
  rounded up = page count; call `onPageCountChange`. Show page `p` by setting
  `scrollLeft = p * (width + 48)` on the container (overflow hidden). Re-measure on resize and when
  font, size or spacing change.
- If `searchQuery` is non-empty: wrap matching text (case-insensitive) in `<mark>`; when the query
  changes, jump to the page containing the first match (find the first `<mark>` element, compute its
  `offsetLeft`, page = `Math.floor(offsetLeft / (width + 48))`).
- Words are clickable (for bookmarks): wrap each token in a `<span data-word={index}>` and handle
  clicks on the paragraph with event delegation reading `data-word`.

Create `components/modes/index.tsx` exporting a `ModeRenderer` that switches on `prefs.reading_mode`
and renders the matching component.

**Check:** typecheck passes. Commit: `reading-center: five reading modes`.

Props: `open, onOpenChange, value, onSave(v)`. Title "Reading Speed (WPM)". Local state starts at
`value` whenever the dialog opens. Big centred number. Left column buttons "-10", "-100"; right column
"+10", "+100". Each clamps to `WPM_MIN..WPM_MAX`. Footer: "Cancel" (closes, discards) and "Save"
(calls `onSave(local)` then closes).

### 11b. `PreferencesDialog.tsx`
Props: `open, onOpenChange, prefs, onChange(partial)`. Title "Preferences".
- If `prefs.reading_mode === "ereader"`: **no tabs**. Show: Reading Mode select, Theme select, Font select
  + Size select on one row, a "Sample Text" preview (rendered in the chosen font and size), and a
  Line Spacing slider (1.0 to 2.0, step 0.1, value shown at right).
- Otherwise tabs **Spreed**, **Appearance**, **Advanced**:
  - Spreed: Reading Mode select, then by mode:
    - serial: "Number of Lines" slider 1–6, "Words Per Line" slider 1–6, "Center Text" switch.
    - flow: "Words Per Line" slider 1–6.
    - highlight: "Words Per Line" slider 1–6, "Number of Lines" slider 1–6.
    - scroll: "Words Per Line" slider 1–6.
  - Appearance: Theme select, Font select + Size select, "Sample Text" preview. This tab must show its
    controls in every mode.
  - Advanced: "Punctuation Pauses" switch with a "?" tooltip: "Slight pause at the end of sentences and
    punctuation marks. Only works with one word at a time." "Speed Variability" switch with tooltip:
    "Gives longer words a little more time and shorter words a little less, at the same overall speed."
- Each slider shows its label at the bottom left and the current value at the bottom right.
- Each font option in the Font select renders in its own font (`style={{ fontFamily: stack }}`).
- Footer button "Restore Defaults": opens a confirm (`AlertDialog`) "Restore all reading settings to
  their defaults?" with "Cancel" / "Restore". On Restore call `onChange({ ...DEFAULT_PREFERENCES,
  goal_wpm: prefs.goal_wpm })` (keep the goal).
- Every change calls `onChange` immediately (live preview). The parent debounces saving (step 12).

Props: `open, onOpenChange, title, tokens, currentWord, wpm, remainingText, onJump(wordIndex), onPlay()`.
Full-screen overlay (Dialog with `max-w-none w-screen h-screen`). Shows: title, a small badge
"Passage", a search input "Search this text...". Body: paragraphs of clickable words; the current word
has a 2px outline in the primary colour; words matching the search (case-insensitive, punctuation
ignored) get a yellow background. Clicking a word calls `onJump(index)` and closes. A round play button

Each row: a snippet of up to 6 words before and 6 after the bookmarked word with that word bold, the

## Step 12: Reader page (`pages/ReaderPage.tsx`)

Route param `slug` (`useParams`). Steps inside the component, in this order:

1. Load `usePassageBySlug(slug)`, `usePreferences()`, `useProgressList()`, `useBookmarks(passage?.id)`.
   While loading show a centred spinner. If the passage is not found show "Passage not found" and a
   link back to `/reading-center`.
2. `tokens = useMemo(() => tokenize(passage.body), [passage.body])`.
3. Keep `prefs` in local state initialised from the query. `updatePrefs(partial)` sets local state
   immediately and calls `useSavePreferences().mutate(partial)` debounced by 500 ms (use a ref holding a
   `setTimeout` id; merge pending partials).
4. Keep the current **word index** in a ref and state (`wordIndex`). This is the single source of truth
   for position.
5. `chunks = useMemo(() => chunkTokens(tokens, prefs.words_per_line, prefs.reading_mode === "serial" ||
   prefs.reading_mode === "highlight" ? prefs.number_of_lines : 1), [...])`.
6. `durations = useMemo(() => chunkDurations(chunks, prefs), [chunks, prefs.wpm,
   prefs.punctuation_pauses, prefs.speed_variability])`.
7. `useReadingClock({ durations, chunkCount: chunks.length, initialChunk: chunkIndexForWord(chunks,
   wordIndex), onChunkChange: c => setWordIndex(chunks[c].startWord), onFinish })`.
   When `chunks` changes (settings change), call `clock.goTo(chunkIndexForWord(chunks, wordIndexRef.current))`
   so the reader keeps their place.
8. Resume: on first load, if saved progress for this passage has `word_index > 0` and no `completed_at`,
   show `ResumePrompt`. Resume sets the word index to the saved value; Start over sets 0.
9. Save progress: debounce 2 s after every `wordIndex` change, and immediately on pause, on finish and on
   unmount: `useSaveProgress().mutate({ passage_id, word_index })`.
10. Record a session (`useRecordSession`) when: the user pauses after at least 5000 ms of active time
    since the last recorded session, on finish, and on unmount if active time ≥ 5000 ms. Fields:
    `words_read` = words advanced during that stretch, `active_ms`, `paused_ms`, `wpm`, `reading_mode`,
    `started_at`, `ended_at`. Reset the stretch counters after recording.
11. `onFinish`: pause, save progress with `completed: true`, record the session, show `CompletionCard`.
    "Read again" sets word 0 and hides the card; "Back to library" navigates to `/reading-center`.
12. Layout:
    - Wrap everything in `ReaderSurface`.
    - Top bar (`h-14 flex items-center px-4`): left a "Back" button (arrow icon + "Back") to
      `/reading-center`; centre the passage title; right icon buttons in this order: Search (only in
      e-Reader; toggles an input in the top bar that sets `searchQuery`), Fullscreen, Bookmarks, Settings.
      Every icon button has an `aria-label`.
    - Stage: `ModeRenderer` (flex-1).
    - Transport (hidden in e-Reader): previous-chunk button (`aria-label="Previous"`), a large round
      play/pause button (`aria-label` "Play" or "Pause"), next-chunk button (`aria-label="Next"`).
    - Bottom bar (`h-12 border-t`): left `ShortcutsPopover`; centre a WPM button showing a gauge icon and
      the number (opens `WpmDialog`) and a "View" button (opens `ViewPanel`); right the text
      `${percentRead}% - ${formatRemaining(remainingMs(durations, clock.chunk))}`.
    - e-Reader bottom bar instead: a page `Slider` (0..pageCount-1), "n / total", previous/next page
      buttons.
13. Fullscreen: `document.documentElement.requestFullscreen()` / `document.exitFullscreen()`; track
    `document.fullscreenElement` with a `fullscreenchange` listener; swap the icon (Maximize / Minimize
    from lucide-react).
14. Bookmarks: `onWordClick(index)` from a mode: if playing, do nothing in Serial/Flow and jump in
    Highlight/Scroll. If paused, add a bookmark at that index and show a toast "Bookmark added" (use the
    app's existing toast: search `src/components/ui/` for `sonner` or `use-toast` and use whichever
    exists). In e-Reader, clicking a word adds a bookmark.
15. Keyboard: one `keydown` listener on `window`. Ignore events when `event.target` is an `input`,
    `textarea`, `select` or has `contenteditable`. Also ignore when any dialog is open. Map:
    - `ArrowUp` or `+` or `=`: wpm +10 (clamped), save.
    - `ArrowDown` or `-`: wpm −10 (clamped), save.
    - `p`/`P` or `" "` (Space): toggle play; call `event.preventDefault()` for Space.
    - `ArrowRight`: next chunk (e-Reader: next page). `ArrowLeft`: previous chunk (e-Reader: previous page).
    - `r`/`R`: restart. `s`/`S`: open settings.
16. Mode switch keeps the word: changing `reading_mode` only changes `prefs`; step 7 re-snaps the chunk.

**Check:** typecheck passes. Commit: `reading-center: reader page`.

## Step 14: Stats page (`pages/StatsPage.tsx`)

1. Header "Your reading" and a "Set goal" button.
2. Period select using `STAT_PERIODS` (default 30 Days). `sinceIso = new Date(Date.now() - ms).toISOString()`.
3. Load `useSessions(sinceIso)` and `usePreferences()`.
4. **Vital stats**: two semicircle gauges built with recharts `PieChart` (startAngle 180, endAngle 0,
   range 0–2000): "Current Speed" = median `effectiveWpm` of the last 5 sessions (0 if none), and
   "Target Speed" = `prefs.goal_wpm`. Show the number under each gauge.
5. Metric select: "Reading Speed", "Reading Focus", "Training Time". One recharts `ResponsiveContainer`
   chart below:
   - Reading Speed: `LineChart`, x = session date, y = effective WPM.
   - Reading Focus: `BarChart`, x = day, y = `active_ms / (active_ms + paused_ms) * 100` averaged per day (%).
   - Training Time: `BarChart`, x = day, y = sum of `active_ms` per day in minutes.
6. Empty state when there are no sessions: a chart icon, "Not enough data yet", "Read a few passages and
   come back."
7. **Goal dialog** (`components/GoalDialog.tsx`): slider 50–5000 step 50, value shown, text "The average
   reader manages about 200 words per minute. A good first goal is 600." Buttons "Cancel", "Recommend"
   (sets the slider to `max(300, round50(3 × currentSpeed))`, or 600 when currentSpeed is 0), "Set"
   (saves `goal_wpm`).

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import type { Preferences, ReaderTheme } from "../lib/types";
import { FONTS, THEME_CLASSES } from "../lib/constants";

/** Apply to the element that holds the passage text (mode renderers, View panel body, e-Reader). */
export const READING_TEXT_STYLE: React.CSSProperties = {
  fontFamily: "var(--rc-font-family)",
  fontSize: "var(--rc-font-size)",
  lineHeight: "var(--rc-line-height)",
};

export interface ReaderSurfaceProps {
  prefs: Preferences;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export function ReaderSurface({ prefs, children, className = "" }: ReaderSurfaceProps): React.ReactElement {
  const [systemIsDark, setSystemIsDark] = useState<boolean>(() => {
    if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.matchMedia) {
      return window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches;
    }
    return false;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined" || !window.matchMedia) return;
    const media = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
    const handler = (e: MediaQueryListEvent) => {
      setSystemIsDark(e.matches);
    };
    media.addEventListener("change", handler);
    return () => {
      media.removeEventListener("change", handler);
    };
  }, []);

  const resolvedTheme: Exclude<ReaderTheme, "system"> = useMemo(() => {
    if (prefs.theme === "system") {
      return systemIsDark ? "dark" : "light";
    }
    return prefs.theme;
  }, [prefs.theme, systemIsDark]);

  const fontStack = useMemo(() => {
    const match = FONTS.find((f) => f.label.toLowerCase() === prefs.font_family.toLowerCase());
    return match ? match.stack : "Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif";
  }, [prefs.font_family]);

  const themeClass = THEME_CLASSES[resolvedTheme] ?? THEME_CLASSES.light;

  return (
    <div
      data-theme={resolvedTheme}
      className={`min-h-screen w-full ${themeClass} ${className}`}
      // Reading styles are exposed as CSS variables, NOT applied to the whole page, so the top bar,
      // buttons and dialogs keep normal app sizing. Only the reading text uses them, via READING_TEXT_STYLE.
      style={{
        ["--rc-font-family" as string]: fontStack,
        ["--rc-font-size" as string]: `${prefs.font_size}px`,
        ["--rc-line-height" as string]: String(prefs.line_spacing),
      } as React.CSSProperties}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}
import type { Token, Chunk, Preferences } from "../../lib/types";

export interface ModeProps {
  tokens: Token[];
  chunks: Chunk[];
  chunk: number;              // current chunk index
  prefs: Preferences;
  playing: boolean;
  onWordClick: (wordIndex: number) => void; // used for jump and bookmarks
}

export interface EReaderModeProps extends ModeProps {
  page: number;
  onPageCountChange: (count: number) => void;
  onPageChange: (page: number) => void;
  searchQuery: string;
}
import React from "react";
import type { ModeProps, EReaderModeProps } from "./types";
import { SerialMode } from "./SerialMode";
import { FlowMode } from "./FlowMode";
import { HighlightMode } from "./HighlightMode";
import { ScrollMode } from "./ScrollMode";
import { EReaderMode } from "./EReaderMode";

export interface ModeRendererProps extends ModeProps {
  page?: number;
  onPageCountChange?: (count: number) => void;
  onPageChange?: (page: number) => void;
  searchQuery?: string;
}

export function ModeRenderer(props: ModeRendererProps): React.ReactElement {
  switch (props.prefs.reading_mode) {
    case "serial":
      return <SerialMode {...props} />;
    case "flow":
      return <FlowMode {...props} />;
    case "highlight":
      return <HighlightMode {...props} />;
    case "scroll":
      return <ScrollMode {...props} />;
    case "ereader":
      return (
        <EReaderMode
          {...props}
          page={props.page ?? 0}
          onPageCountChange={props.onPageCountChange ?? (() => {})}
          onPageChange={props.onPageChange ?? (() => {})}
          searchQuery={props.searchQuery ?? ""}
        />
      );
    default:
      return <SerialMode {...props} />;
  }
}

import React from "react";
import type { ModeProps } from "./types";

export function SerialMode({
  chunks,
  chunk,
  prefs,
  playing,
  onWordClick,
}: ModeProps): React.ReactElement {
  const currentChunk = chunks[chunk];
  if (!currentChunk) {
    return <div className="flex-1" />;
  }

  const containerClasses = prefs.center_text
    ? "flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center items-center text-center w-full px-6"
    : "flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center items-start text-left w-full pl-[15%] pr-6";

  return (
    <div className={containerClasses}>
      {currentChunk.rows.map((row, rowIdx) => (
        <div key={rowIdx} className="leading-tight py-1">
          {row.map((token, tokenIdx) => (
            <React.Fragment key={token.index}>
              {tokenIdx > 0 && " "}
              {!playing ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onWordClick(token.index)}
                  className="cursor-pointer hover:underline focus:outline-none bg-transparent border-0 p-0 font-inherit text-inherit"
                >
                  {token.text}
                </button>
              ) : (
                <span>{token.text}</span>
              )}
            </React.Fragment>
          ))}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
import React, { useMemo } from "react";
import type { ModeProps } from "./types";
import { chunkTokens } from "../../lib/tokenize";

export function FlowMode({
  tokens,
  chunks,
  chunk,
  prefs,
  playing,
  onWordClick,
}: ModeProps): React.ReactElement {
  const currentChunk = chunks[chunk];

  // Group tokens into lines of words_per_line tokens, never crossing paragraph
  const lines = useMemo(() => {
    return chunkTokens(tokens, prefs.words_per_line, 1);
  }, [tokens, prefs.words_per_line]);

  if (!currentChunk) {
    return <div className="flex-1" />;
  }

  // Find the line containing the current chunk's start word
  const currentLine = lines.find(
    (l) => currentChunk.startWord >= l.startWord && currentChunk.startWord <= l.endWord,
  ) ?? lines[0];

  const lineTokens = currentLine?.rows[0] ?? [];

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center items-start text-left w-full pl-[15%] pr-6">
      <div className="leading-tight py-1">
        {lineTokens.map((token, tokenIdx) => {
          const isCurrentWord =
            token.index >= currentChunk.startWord && token.index <= currentChunk.endWord;

          return (
            <React.Fragment key={token.index}>
              {tokenIdx > 0 && " "}
              <span className={isCurrentWord ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0 select-none pointer-events-none"}>
                {!playing && isCurrentWord ? (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onWordClick(token.index)}
                    className="cursor-pointer hover:underline focus:outline-none bg-transparent border-0 p-0 font-inherit text-inherit"
                  >
                    {token.text}
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <span>{token.text}</span>
                )}
              </span>
            </React.Fragment>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
import React, { useMemo, useRef, useEffect } from "react";
import type { ModeProps } from "./types";
import { chunkTokens } from "../../lib/tokenize";

export function HighlightMode({
  tokens,
  chunks,
  chunk,
  prefs,
  playing,
  onWordClick,
}: ModeProps): React.ReactElement {
  const currentChunk = chunks[chunk];
  const activeWordRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement | null>(null);

  // Group tokens into lines of words_per_line tokens, never crossing a paragraph boundary
  const lines = useMemo(() => {
    return chunkTokens(tokens, prefs.words_per_line, 1);
  }, [tokens, prefs.words_per_line]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeWordRef.current) {
      activeWordRef.current.scrollIntoView({
        block: "center",
        behavior: prefs.wpm > 600 ? "auto" : "smooth",
      });
    }
  }, [chunk, prefs.wpm]);

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 w-full overflow-y-auto px-6 py-12 flex justify-center">
      <div className="max-w-[46rem] w-full">
        {lines.map((line, lineIdx) => {
          const rowTokens = line.rows[0] ?? [];
          const endsParagraph = rowTokens[rowTokens.length - 1]?.endsParagraph;

          return (
            <div
              key={lineIdx}
              className={`leading-relaxed py-1 ${endsParagraph ? "mb-6" : ""}`}
            >
              {rowTokens.map((token, tokenIdx) => {
                const isCurrentWord =
                  currentChunk &&
                  token.index >= currentChunk.startWord &&
                  token.index <= currentChunk.endWord;
                const isFirstOfCurrent =
                  currentChunk && token.index === currentChunk.startWord;

                return (
                  <React.Fragment key={token.index}>
                    {tokenIdx > 0 && " "}
                    <span
                      ref={isFirstOfCurrent ? activeWordRef : null}
                      style={{ opacity: isCurrentWord ? 1 : 0.35 }}
                      className={isCurrentWord ? "font-semibold" : ""}
                    >
                      {!playing ? (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => onWordClick(token.index)}
                          className="cursor-pointer hover:underline focus:outline-none bg-transparent border-0 p-0 font-inherit text-inherit"
                        >
                          {token.text}
                        </button>
                      ) : (
                        <span>{token.text}</span>
                      )}
                    </span>
                  </React.Fragment>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
import React, { useMemo, useRef, useState, useEffect } from "react";
import type { ModeProps } from "./types";
import { chunkTokens } from "../../lib/tokenize";

export function ScrollMode({
  tokens,
  chunks,
  chunk,
  prefs,
  playing,
  onWordClick,
}: ModeProps): React.ReactElement {
  const currentChunk = chunks[chunk];
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const rowRefs = useRef<Map<number, HTMLDivElement>>(new Map());
  const [translateY, setTranslateY] = useState<number>(0);

  // Group tokens into lines of words_per_line tokens, never crossing a paragraph boundary
  const lines = useMemo(() => {
    return chunkTokens(tokens, prefs.words_per_line, 1);
  }, [tokens, prefs.words_per_line]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!currentChunk || !containerRef.current) return;
    const lineIndex = lines.findIndex(
      (l) => currentChunk.startWord >= l.startWord && currentChunk.startWord <= l.endWord,
    );
    if (lineIndex === -1) return;
    const rowEl = rowRefs.current.get(lineIndex);
    if (!rowEl) return;

    const containerHeight = containerRef.current.clientHeight;
    const targetY = containerHeight * 0.4;
    const rowOffset = rowEl.offsetTop;
    setTranslateY(targetY - rowOffset);
  }, [chunk, currentChunk, lines]);

  return (
    <div
      ref={containerRef}
      className="relative flex-1 w-full max-w-[46rem] mx-auto h-[70vh] overflow-hidden px-6"
    >
      {/* 40% horizontal guide line */}
      <div
        className="absolute left-0 right-0 border-t border-current opacity-30 pointer-events-none z-10"
        style={{ top: "40%" }}
      />

      {/* Inner column translated */}
      <div
        className="w-full will-change-transform"
        style={{
          transform: `translateY(${translateY}px)`,
          transition: "transform 150ms linear",
        }}
      >
        {lines.map((line, lineIdx) => {
          const rowTokens = line.rows[0] ?? [];
          const endsParagraph = rowTokens[rowTokens.length - 1]?.endsParagraph;

          return (
            <div
              key={lineIdx}
              ref={(el) => {
                if (el) rowRefs.current.set(lineIdx, el);
                else rowRefs.current.delete(lineIdx);
              }}
              className={`leading-relaxed py-1 ${endsParagraph ? "mb-6" : ""}`}
            >
              {rowTokens.map((token, tokenIdx) => {
                const isCurrentWord =
                  currentChunk &&
                  token.index >= currentChunk.startWord &&
                  token.index <= currentChunk.endWord;

                return (
                  <React.Fragment key={token.index}>
                    {tokenIdx > 0 && " "}
                    <span
                      style={{ opacity: isCurrentWord ? 1 : 0.6 }}
                      className={isCurrentWord ? "font-semibold" : ""}
                    >
                      {!playing ? (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => onWordClick(token.index)}
                          className="cursor-pointer hover:underline focus:outline-none bg-transparent border-0 p-0 font-inherit text-inherit"
                        >
                          {token.text}
                        </button>
                      ) : (
                        <span>{token.text}</span>
                      )}
                    </span>
                  </React.Fragment>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
import React, { useMemo, useRef, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import type { EReaderModeProps } from "./types";
import { splitParagraphs } from "../../lib/tokenize";

export function EReaderMode({
  tokens,
  prefs,
  page,
  onPageCountChange,
  onPageChange,
  searchQuery,
  onWordClick,
}: EReaderModeProps): React.ReactElement {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

  // Group tokens by paragraph
  const paragraphs = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<number, typeof tokens>();
    for (const token of tokens) {
      const list = map.get(token.paragraphIndex) ?? [];
      list.push(token);
      map.set(token.paragraphIndex, list);
    }
    return Array.from(map.values());
  }, [tokens]);

  const measurePagination = useCallback(() => {
    if (!containerRef.current) return;
    const width = containerRef.current.clientWidth;
    if (width <= 0) return;
    const scrollWidth = containerRef.current.scrollWidth;
    const pageWidth = width + 48;
    const count = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(scrollWidth / pageWidth));
    onPageCountChange(count);
  }, [onPageCountChange]);

  // Re-measure on resize and when font, size or line spacing change
  useEffect(() => {
    measurePagination();
    const handleResize = () => measurePagination();
    window.addEventListener("resize", handleResize);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("resize", handleResize);
  }, [measurePagination, prefs.font_family, prefs.font_size, prefs.line_spacing]);

  // Update scrollLeft when page changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!containerRef.current) return;
    const width = containerRef.current.clientWidth;
    const pageWidth = width + 48;
    containerRef.current.scrollLeft = page * pageWidth;
  }, [page]);

  // Handle search query jumps
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!searchQuery.trim() || !containerRef.current) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (!containerRef.current) return;
      const firstMark = containerRef.current.querySelector("mark");
      if (firstMark) {
        const offsetLeft = (firstMark as HTMLElement).offsetLeft;
        const width = containerRef.current.clientWidth;
        const pageWidth = width + 48;
        const targetPage = Math.floor(offsetLeft / pageWidth);
        onPageChange(targetPage);
      }
    }, 50);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [searchQuery, onPageChange]);

  const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    const target = (e.target as HTMLElement).closest("[data-word]");
    if (target) {
      const wordAttr = target.getAttribute("data-word");
      if (wordAttr !== null) {
        onWordClick(Number(wordAttr));
      }
    }
  };

  const renderTokenText = (text: string) => {
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) {
      return text;
    }
    const escapedQuery = searchQuery.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
    const regex = new RegExp(`(${escapedQuery})`, "gi");
    const parts = text.split(regex);

    return parts.map((part, i) =>
      regex.test(part) ? (
        <mark key={i} className="bg-yellow-300 text-black rounded-xs px-0.5">
          {part}
        </mark>
      ) : (
        part
      ),
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 w-full max-w-[46rem] mx-auto px-6 overflow-hidden">
      <div
        ref={containerRef}
        onClick={handleClick}
        className="w-full overflow-hidden select-text"
        style={{
          height: "calc(100vh - 140px)",
          columnWidth: "calc(100% - 0px)",
          columnGap: "48px",
          columnFill: "auto",
        }}
      >
        {paragraphs.map((paraTokens, pIdx) => (
          <p key={pIdx} className="mb-6 leading-relaxed">
            {paraTokens.map((token, tIdx) => (
              <React.Fragment key={token.index}>
                {tIdx > 0 && " "}
                <span
                  data-word={token.index}
                  className="cursor-pointer hover:underline"
                >
                  {renderTokenText(token.text)}
                </span>
              </React.Fragment>
            ))}
          </p>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export type ReadingMode = "serial" | "flow" | "highlight" | "scroll" | "ereader";
export type ReaderTheme = "light" | "dark" | "high_contrast" | "system";

export interface Passage {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  author: string | null;
  body: string;
  cover_url: string | null;
  word_count: number;
  sort_order: number;
  is_published: boolean;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export interface Preferences {
  wpm: number;
  reading_mode: ReadingMode;
  number_of_lines: number;
  words_per_line: number;
  center_text: boolean;
  theme: ReaderTheme;
  font_family: string;
  font_size: number;
  line_spacing: number;
  punctuation_pauses: boolean;
  speed_variability: boolean;
  goal_wpm: number;
}

export interface Progress {
  passage_id: string;
  word_index: number;
  completed_at: string | null;
  last_opened_at: string;
  hidden_from_recent: boolean;
}

export interface Bookmark {
  id: string;
  passage_id: string;
  word_index: number;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface ReadingSession {
  id: string;
  passage_id: string;
  reading_mode: ReadingMode;
  wpm: number;
  words_read: number;
  active_ms: number;
  paused_ms: number;
  started_at: string;
  ended_at: string;
}

export interface Token {
  index: number;          // position in the whole passage, starting at 0
  text: string;           // the word with its punctuation attached
  paragraphIndex: number; // starting at 0
  endsSentence: boolean;
  hasPunctuation: boolean;
  endsParagraph: boolean;
}

export interface Chunk {
  index: number;          // chunk number, starting at 0
  startWord: number;      // token index of the first word
  endWord: number;        // token index of the last word (inclusive)
  rows: Token[][];        // numberOfLines rows, each up to wordsPerLine tokens
}

# Reading Center

Speed-reading tool for general articles (not LSAT RC). Spec: ai-os
brainstorms/2026-10-12-reading-center-build-spec.md.

- lib/: pure logic (tokenize, timing, constants, types). No React, no Supabase. Unit tested.
- hooks/: data access (react-query) and the reading clock.
- components/: UI. components/modes/ holds one renderer per reading mode.
- pages/: route components (library, reader, stats, admin).

Word lists (next phase) will link to (passage_id, word_index). Word indexes come from
lib/tokenize.ts and must stay stable for a given passage body.

import { useState, useRef, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";

export function useReadingClock(args: {
  durations: number[];        // from chunkDurations
  chunkCount: number;
  initialChunk: number;
  onChunkChange: (chunk: number) => void;
  onFinish: () => void;
}): {
  playing: boolean;
  chunk: number;
  play: () => void;
  pause: () => void;
  toggle: () => void;
  goTo: (chunk: number) => void;   // clamps to 0..chunkCount-1, keeps playing state
  next: () => void;
  prev: () => void;
  restart: () => void;             // goTo(0) and pause
  activeMs: () => number;          // total ms spent playing since mount
  pausedMs: () => number;          // total ms spent paused since the first play
} {
  const [chunk, setChunk] = useState<number>(args.initialChunk);
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const chunkRef = useRef<number>(args.initialChunk);
  const playingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const durationsRef = useRef<number[]>(args.durations);
  const chunkCountRef = useRef<number>(args.chunkCount);
  const onChunkChangeRef = useRef<(chunk: number) => void>(args.onChunkChange);
  const onFinishRef = useRef<() => void>(args.onFinish);

  const animFrameIdRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const elapsedInChunkRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const activeMsRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const pausedMsRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const hasPlayedOnceRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const pauseStartRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const lastFrameRef = useRef<number>(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    durationsRef.current = args.durations;
    chunkCountRef.current = args.chunkCount;
    onChunkChangeRef.current = args.onChunkChange;
    onFinishRef.current = args.onFinish;
  });

  const tick = useCallback((now: number) => {
    if (!playingRef.current) return;

    let delta = now - lastFrameRef.current;
    lastFrameRef.current = now;
    if (delta > 1000) delta = 1000;
    if (delta < 0) delta = 0;

    elapsedInChunkRef.current += delta;
    activeMsRef.current += delta;

    let currentChunk = chunkRef.current;
    const durations = durationsRef.current;
    const count = chunkCountRef.current;

    let advanced = false;
    while (
      currentChunk < count &&
      elapsedInChunkRef.current >= (durations[currentChunk] ?? 0)
    ) {
      elapsedInChunkRef.current -= (durations[currentChunk] ?? 0);
      currentChunk++;
      advanced = true;
    }

    if (currentChunk >= count) {
      const lastChunk = Math.max(0, count - 1);
      chunkRef.current = lastChunk;
      setChunk(lastChunk);
      playingRef.current = false;
      setPlaying(false);
      animFrameIdRef.current = null;
      pauseStartRef.current = performance.now();
      if (advanced) {
        onChunkChangeRef.current(lastChunk);
      }
      onFinishRef.current();
      return;
    }

    if (advanced) {
      chunkRef.current = currentChunk;
      setChunk(currentChunk);
      onChunkChangeRef.current(currentChunk);
    }

    animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }, []);

  const play = useCallback(() => {
    if (playingRef.current) return;
    const count = chunkCountRef.current;
    if (count <= 0) return;

    if (pauseStartRef.current !== null && hasPlayedOnceRef.current) {
      pausedMsRef.current += performance.now() - pauseStartRef.current;
      pauseStartRef.current = null;
    }
    hasPlayedOnceRef.current = true;
    playingRef.current = true;
    setPlaying(true);
    lastFrameRef.current = performance.now();
    animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }, [tick]);

  const pause = useCallback(() => {
    if (!playingRef.current) return;
    playingRef.current = false;
    setPlaying(false);
    if (animFrameIdRef.current !== null) {
      cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameIdRef.current);
      animFrameIdRef.current = null;
    }
    pauseStartRef.current = performance.now();
  }, []);

  const toggle = useCallback(() => {
    if (playingRef.current) {
      pause();
    } else {
      play();
    }
  }, [pause, play]);

  const goTo = useCallback((target: number) => {
    const count = chunkCountRef.current;
    const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(count - 1, target));
    elapsedInChunkRef.current = 0;
    if (chunkRef.current !== clamped) {
      chunkRef.current = clamped;
      setChunk(clamped);
      onChunkChangeRef.current(clamped);
    }
  }, []);

  const next = useCallback(() => {
    goTo(chunkRef.current + 1);
  }, [goTo]);

  const prev = useCallback(() => {
    goTo(chunkRef.current - 1);
  }, [goTo]);

  const restart = useCallback(() => {
    pause();
    goTo(0);
  }, [pause, goTo]);

  const activeMs = useCallback((): number => {
    return Math.round(activeMsRef.current);
  }, []);

  const pausedMs = useCallback((): number => {
    let total = pausedMsRef.current;
    if (!playingRef.current && hasPlayedOnceRef.current && pauseStartRef.current !== null) {
      total += performance.now() - pauseStartRef.current;
    }
    return Math.round(total);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (animFrameIdRef.current !== null) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameIdRef.current);
      }
    };
  }, []);

  return {
    playing,
    chunk,
    play,
    pause,
    toggle,
    goTo,
    next,
    prev,
    restart,
    activeMs,
    pausedMs,
  };
}

      return (data ?? []) as Progress[];
    },
    enabled: !!userId,
  });
}

export interface SaveProgressInput {
  passage_id: string;
  word_index: number;
  completed?: boolean;
  hidden_from_recent?: boolean;
}

export function useSaveProgress(): UseMutationResult<void, Error, SaveProgressInput> {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const userId = user?.id;
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: SaveProgressInput): Promise<void> => {
      if (!userId) throw new Error("Not authenticated");
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const payload: {
        user_id: string;
        passage_id: string;
        word_index: number;
        last_opened_at: string;
        completed_at?: string;
        hidden_from_recent?: boolean;
      } = {
        user_id: userId,
        passage_id: input.passage_id,
        word_index: input.word_index,
        last_opened_at: now,
      };

      if (input.completed === true) {
        payload.completed_at = now;
      }
      if (typeof input.hidden_from_recent === "boolean") {
        payload.hidden_from_recent = input.hidden_from_recent;
      }

      const { error } = await rcDb("reading_center_progress").upsert(payload, {
        onConflict: "user_id,passage_id",
      });
      if (error) throw error;
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      if (userId) {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["reading-center", "progress", userId] });
      }
    },
  });
}

export function useUpsertPassage(): UseMutationResult<
  Passage,
  Error,
  Partial<Passage> & { title: string; body: string }
> {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (
      passage: Partial<Passage> & { title: string; body: string },
    ): Promise<Passage> => {
      const word_count = countWords(passage.body);
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const payload = {
        ...passage,
        word_count,
        updated_at: now,
      };
      const { data, error } = await rcDb("reading_center_passages")
        .upsert(payload)
        .select()
        .single();
      if (error) throw error;
      return data as Passage;
    },
    onSuccess: (_data, vars) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["reading-center", "passages"] });
      if (vars.slug) {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["reading-center", "passage", vars.slug] });
      }
    },
  });
}

export function useDeletePassage(): UseMutationResult<void, Error, string> {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
      const { error } = await rcDb("reading_center_passages")
        .delete()
        .eq("id", id);
      if (error) throw error;
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["reading-center", "passages"] });
    },
  });
}

export function useReorderPassages(): UseMutationResult<void, Error, { id: string; sort_order: number }[]> {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (items: { id: string; sort_order: number }[]): Promise<void> => {
      for (const item of items) {
        const { error } = await rcDb("reading_center_passages")
          .update({ sort_order: item.sort_order })
          .eq("id", item.id);
        if (error) throw error;
      }
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["reading-center", "passages"] });
    },
  });
}
  return varied.map((d) => d * scale);
}

/** Milliseconds left from the start of chunk `fromChunk` to the end. */
export function remainingMs(durations: number[], fromChunk: number): number {
  let sum = 0;
  for (let i = Math.max(0, fromChunk); i < durations.length; i++) sum += durations[i];
  return sum;
}

/** "N minutes remaining", "1 minute remaining" or "Less than a minute remaining". */
export function formatRemaining(ms: number): string {
  if (ms < 60000) return "Less than a minute remaining";
  const m = Math.ceil(ms / 60000);
  return m === 1 ? "1 minute remaining" : `${m} minutes remaining`;
}

/** Whole-number percent of words read, 0 to 100. */
export function percentRead(wordIndex: number, totalWords: number): number {
  if (totalWords <= 0) return 0;
  return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Math.floor((wordIndex / totalWords) * 100)));
}

/** Effective WPM for a session. Returns 0 if no active time. */
export function effectiveWpm(wordsRead: number, activeMs: number): number {
  if (activeMs <= 0) return 0;
  return Math.round(wordsRead / (activeMs / 60000));
}

/** Estimated whole minutes to read `words` at `wpm`, at least 1. */
export function estimateMinutes(words: number, wpm: number): number {
  return Math.max(1, Math.round(words / wpm));
}

import { NavLink, Outlet, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  BarChart3,
  CreditCard,
  Users,
  LayoutGrid,
  Library,
  MessageSquare,
  PencilRuler,
  Sparkles,
  type LucideIcon,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { OperatorChatProvider } from '@/components/teacher/ai-ta/OperatorChatProvider';

type Room = {
  to: string;
  label: string;
  Icon: LucideIcon;
  /** NavLink `end`: only Today, so exactly one item is ever active. */
  end?: boolean;
};

/**
 * The rooms in the teacher workspace. This list is the only top navigation in
 * the teacher workspace: no `?ws=` switching, no second rail,
 * no per-surface tab bars pretending to be nav.
 *
 * `end` is set on Today alone. Every other room owns a path prefix, so its
 * child routes (library tabs, a student dossier, a thread) keep the parent
 * highlighted, and no two items can be active at once.
 */
const ROOMS: Room[] = [
  { to: '/teacher/today', label: 'Today', Icon: LayoutGrid, end: true },
  { to: '/teacher/inbox', label: 'Inbox', Icon: MessageSquare },
  { to: '/teacher/payments', label: 'Payments', Icon: CreditCard },
  { to: '/teacher/students', label: 'Students', Icon: Users },
  { to: '/teacher/library', label: 'Library', Icon: Library },
  { to: '/teacher/assistant', label: 'Teaching Assistant', Icon: Sparkles },
  { to: '/teacher/insights', label: 'Insights', Icon: BarChart3 },
];

/**
 * The perspective floor from the Desk. Kept so the workspace ground reads as
 * the same room the Desk surfaces were designed for.
 */
const FLOOR_GRID_STYLE: React.CSSProperties = {
  transform: 'perspective(440px) rotateX(62deg)',
  transformOrigin: 'top center',
  backgroundImage:
    'linear-gradient(rgba(13,19,22,0.14) 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(13,19,22,0.10) 1px, transparent 1px)',
  backgroundSize: '46px 46px',
  maskImage: 'linear-gradient(to top, black 30%, transparent 88%)',
  WebkitMaskImage: 'linear-gradient(to top, black 30%, transparent 88%)',
};

/**
 * The teacher workspace shell: one top rail that never unmounts, one header,
 * one accent. Every room renders into the `<Outlet/>`; nothing here remounts
          </div>

          {/* The room rail. Hidden below md, where the bottom bar takes over. */}
          <nav
            className="hidden items-stretch gap-1 overflow-x-auto md:flex"
            aria-label="Teacher workspace"
          >
            {ROOMS.map(({ to, label, Icon, end }) => (
              <NavLink
                key={to}
                to={to}
                end={end}
                className={({ isActive }) =>
                  cn(
                    // Deliberately heavier than a room's own tab row below it,
                    // so two stacked horizontal strips can't read as the same
                    // level of navigation.
                    'inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b-[3px] px-3.5 py-3 text-sm font-medium transition',
                    isActive
                      ? 'border-teacher-accent font-semibold text-black'
                      : 'border-transparent text-black/50 hover:text-black',
                  )
                }
              >
                <Icon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.5} />
                <span className="truncate">{label}</span>
              </NavLink>
            ))}
          </nav>
        </header>

        <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1">
          <main className="flex min-h-0 min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col overflow-y-auto">
            <Outlet />
          </main>
        </div>

        {/* Mobile: the sidebar becomes a bottom bar. Admin has to work on a phone. */}
        <nav
          className="flex shrink-0 items-stretch overflow-x-auto border-t border-black/10 bg-[#c3cace] pb-[max(0.25rem,env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] md:hidden"
          aria-label="Teacher workspace"
        >
          {ROOMS.map(({ to, label, Icon, end }) => (
            <NavLink
              key={to}
              to={to}
              end={end}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                cn(
                  'flex min-w-[4.5rem] shrink-0 flex-col items-center gap-0.5 py-2 text-[10px] font-medium transition-colors',
                  isActive ? 'text-black' : 'text-black/45',
                )
              }
            >
              <Icon className="h-5 w-5" strokeWidth={1.5} />
              <span className="max-w-full truncate px-1">{label}</span>
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </nav>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

                        room-to-room navigation is real browser history.

                        It hangs off the ClassroomLayout route deliberately.
                        Opening it from the classroom rail is entering a room,
                        not leaving for another app: the classroom rail stays
                        mounted and the workspace draws into the stage beside
                        it. As its own top-level route it swapped the entire
                        frame out — the same shell-swap Messages and Flagged
                        were moved in here to stop.

                        requireAdmin still guards it; nesting changed the
                        frame, not who can reach it. */}
                    <Route path="/teacher" element={
                      <ProtectedRoute requireAdmin>
                        <React.Suspense fallback={<div className="min-h-screen bg-[#c3cace]" />}>
                          <TeacherWorkspaceShell />
                        </React.Suspense>
                      </ProtectedRoute>
                    }>
                      <Route index element={<TeacherWorkspaceIndex />} />

                      <Route path="today" element={
                        <React.Suspense fallback={<div />}>
                          <DeskTodaySurface />
                        </React.Suspense>
                      } />
                      <Route path="payments" element={
                        <React.Suspense fallback={<div />}>
                          <TeacherPayments />
                        </React.Suspense>
                      } />
                      <Route path="students" element={
                        <React.Suspense fallback={<div />}>
                          <DeskPeopleSurface />
                        </React.Suspense>
                      } />
                      {/* The dossier (T3.2): one URL per student, five real
                          child routes so each surface is linkable and
                          back/forward work. */}
                      <Route path="students/:studentId" element={
                        <React.Suspense fallback={<div />}>
                          <StudentDossier />
                        </React.Suspense>
                      }>
                        <Route index element={<Navigate to="overview" replace />} />
                        <Route path="overview" element={
                          <React.Suspense fallback={<div />}><DossierOverview /></React.Suspense>
                        } />
                        <Route path="work" element={
                          <React.Suspense fallback={<div />}><DossierWork /></React.Suspense>

                        <Route path="review" element={
                          <React.Suspense fallback={<div />}><DossierReview /></React.Suspense>

                        <Route path="progress" element={

                      <Route path="templates" element={

/**
 * Headless assign: put an Activity, a guided drill, or a plain message into a
 * student's LSAT U account from the command line.
 *
 * Run with vite-node so `@/` resolves and this calls the app's OWN assignWork()
 * rather than a second implementation of it. That matters: assigning touches
 * seven tables (assignment snapshot, classroom item, conversation, message,
 * notification, plus the email edge function) and rolls back on failure. A
 * reimplementation would drift from the UI the first time the app changed.
 *
 *   npx vite-node scripts/assign.ts -- --student "Celia" --activity "Read, Recall, Colour"
 *   npx vite-node scripts/assign.ts -- --student "Celia" --drill "main-conclusion-1"
 *   npx vite-node scripts/assign.ts -- --student "Celia" --message "Start with PT105.S2."
 *   npx vite-node scripts/assign.ts -- --student "Celia" --section PT156.S2 --due 2026-09-13
 *   npx vite-node scripts/assign.ts -- --student "Celia" --set "PT156.S2 (LR)"
 *   npx vite-node scripts/assign.ts -- --student "Celia" --activity "..." --dry-run
 *
 * Credentials are read from ~/ai-os/.env (git-ignored), never from this repo's
 * .env, which IS tracked in git. Same convention as scripts/db-pull.mjs.
 */
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { assignWork } from "@/lib/assignWork";
import { BOOTCAMPS } from "@/lib/bootcamps";
import { updateClassroomItem } from "@/lib/classroomItems";

const REPO = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");

function readEnv(file: string): Record<string, string> {
  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return out;
  for (const line of fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8").split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const t = line.trim();
    if (!t || t.startsWith("#")) continue;
    const i = t.indexOf("=");
    if (i < 0) continue;
    out[t.slice(0, i).trim()] = t.slice(i + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");
  }

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- arguments

function arg(name: string): string | undefined {
  const i = process.argv.indexOf(`--${name}`);
  return i >= 0 ? process.argv[i + 1] : undefined;
}
const has = (name: string) => process.argv.includes(`--${name}`);

const studentQuery = arg("student");
const activityQuery = arg("activity");
const drillQuery = arg("drill");
const messageText = arg("message");
const sectionQuery = arg("section");
const setQuery = arg("set");
const bootcampQuery = arg("bootcamp");
const scheduleFor = arg("schedule");
const dueOn = arg("due");
const dryRun = has("dry-run");

const kinds = [activityQuery, drillQuery, messageText, sectionQuery, setQuery, bootcampQuery].filter(Boolean).length;
if (!studentQuery || kinds !== 1) {
  console.error(
    "usage: vite-node scripts/assign.ts -- --student <name|email> \\\n" +
      "         (--activity <title> | --drill <slug|title> | --message <text>\\\n" +
      "          | --section PT<n>.S<n> | --set <title|id> | --bootcamp <id|title>)\\\n" +
      "         [--schedule YYYY-MM-DD] [--due YYYY-MM-DD] [--dry-run]",
  );
  process.exit(2);
}
for (const [flag, v] of [["schedule", scheduleFor], ["due", dueOn]] as const) {
  if (v && !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(v)) {
    console.error(`--${flag} must be YYYY-MM-DD, got "${v}"`);
    process.exit(2);
  }
}
if (dueOn && scheduleFor && dueOn < scheduleFor) {
  console.error(`--due ${dueOn} is before --schedule ${scheduleFor}; she could never do it in time.`);
  process.exit(2);
}

 */

 * Group tokens into chunks of up to wordsPerLine * numberOfLines words.
 * A chunk never crosses a paragraph boundary.
 */
export function chunkTokens(tokens: Token[], wordsPerLine: number, numberOfLines: number): Chunk[] {
  const size = Math.max(1, wordsPerLine) * Math.max(1, numberOfLines);
  const chunks: Chunk[] = [];
  let current: Token[] = [];
  const flush = () => {
    if (current.length === 0) return;
    const rows: Token[][] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < current.length; i += wordsPerLine) rows.push(current.slice(i, i + wordsPerLine));
    chunks.push({
      index: chunks.length,
      startWord: current[0].index,
      endWord: current[current.length - 1].index,
      rows,
    });
    current = [];
  };
  for (const t of tokens) {
    current.push(t);
    if (current.length === size || t.endsParagraph) flush();
  }
  flush();
  return chunks;
}

/** Index of the chunk that contains wordIndex. Returns 0 if not found. */
export function chunkIndexForWord(chunks: Chunk[], wordIndex: number): number {
  const i = chunks.findIndex((c) => wordIndex >= c.startWord && wordIndex <= c.endWord);
  return i === -1 ? 0 : i;

Words outside the\n * current chunk are invisible but keep their space.\n */\nexport function FlowMode({ tokens, chunks, chunk, prefs, playing, onWordClick }: ModeProps): React.ReactElement {\n  const currentChunk = chunks[chunk];\n  const lines = useMemo(\n    () => chunkTokens(tokens, flowLineWords(prefs.words_per_line), 1),\n    [tokens, prefs.words_per_line],\n  );\n\n  if (!currentChunk) return <div className=\"flex-1\" />;\n\n  const currentLine =\n    lines.find((l) => currentChunk.startWord >= l.startWord && currentChunk.startWord <= l.endWord) ?? lines[0];\n  const lineTokens = currentLine?.rows[0] ?? [];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center items-start text-left w-full pl-[15%] pr-6\">\n      <div style={READING_TEXT_STYLE}>\n        {lineTokens.map((token, tokenIdx) => {\n          const visible = token.index >= currentChunk.startWord && token.index <= currentChunk.endWord;\n          return (\n            <React.Fragment key={token.index}>\n              {tokenIdx > 0 && \" \"}\n              <span className={visible ? \"\" : \"opacity-0 select-none pointer-events-none\"} aria-hidden={!visible}>\n                <Word token={token} clickable={!playing && visible} onWordClick={onWordClick} />\n              </span>\n            </React.Fragment>\n          );\n        })}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"}

{"file_path": "C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/src/features/reading-center/components/modes/ScrollMode.tsx", "content": "import React, { useMemo, useRef, useState, useLayoutEffect } from \"react\";\nimport type { ModeProps } from \"./types\";\nimport { chunkTokens } from \"../../lib/tokenize\";\nimport { READING_TEXT_STYLE } from \"../ReaderSurface\";\nimport { Word } from \"./Word\";\n\nexport function ScrollMode({ tokens, chunks, chunk, prefs, playing, onWordClick }: ModeProps): React.ReactElement {\n  const currentChunk = chunks[chunk];\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const rowRefs = useRef<Map<number, HTMLDivElement>>(new Map());\n  const [translateY, setTranslateY] = useState<number>(0);\n\n  const lines = useMemo(() => chunkTokens(tokens, prefs.words_per_line, 1), [tokens, prefs.words_per_line]);\n\n  const currentLineIndex = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!currentChunk) return -1;\n    return lines.findIndex((l) => currentChunk.startWord >= l.startWord && currentChunk.startWord <= l.endWord);\n  }, [lines, currentChunk]);\n\n  // Put the current row on the guide line. Re-measure when the text reflows.\n  useLayoutEffect(() => {\n    const measure = () => {\n      const container = containerRef.current;\n      const rowEl = rowRefs.current.get(currentLineIndex);\n      if (!container || !rowEl) return;\n      setTranslateY(container.clientHeight * 0.4 - rowEl.offsetTop);\n    };\n    measure();\n    window.addEventListener(\"resize\", measure);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener(\"resize\", measure);\n  }, [currentLineIndex, prefs.font_size, prefs.line_spacing, prefs.font_family]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex-1 min-h-0 w-full flex items-center\">\n      <div ref={containerRef} className=\"relative w-full max-w-[46rem] mx-auto h-[70vh] overflow-hidden px-6\">\n        <div\n          aria-hidden\n          className=\"absolute left-0 right-0 border-t border-current opacity-30 pointer-events-none z-10\"\n          style={{ top: \"40%\" }}\n        />\n        <div\n          className=\"w-full will-change-transform\"\n          style={{ ...READING_TEXT_STYLE, transform: `translateY(${translateY}px)`, transition: \"transform 150ms linear\" }}\n        >\n          {lines.map((line, lineIdx) => {\n            const rowTokens = line.rows[0] ?? [];\n            const endsParagraph = rowTokens[rowTokens.length - 1]?.endsParagraph;\n            return (\n              <div\n                key={lineIdx}\n                ref={(el) => {\n                  if (el) rowRefs.current.set(lineIdx, el);\n                  else rowRefs.current.delete(lineIdx);\n                }}\n                className={endsParagraph ? \"mb-6\" : \"\"}\n              >\n                {rowTokens.map((token, tokenIdx) => {\n                  const current =\n                    !!currentChunk && token.index >= currentChunk.startWord && token.index <= currentChunk.endWord;\n                  return (\n                    <React.Fragment key={token.index}>\n                      {tokenIdx > 0 && \" \"}\n                      <span style={{ opacity: current ? 1 : 0.6 }} className={current ? \"font-semibold\" : \"\"}>\n                        <Word token={token} clickable={!playing} onWordClick={onWordClick} />\n                      </span>\n                    </React.Fragment>\n                  );\n                })}\n              </div>\n            );\n          })}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"}

The text is laid out in CSS columns\n * exactly one container wide, so each column is one page; turning a page sets\n * scrollLeft to page * (width + gap).\n */\nexport function EReaderMode({\n  tokens,\n  prefs,\n  page,\n  onPageCountChange,\n  onPageChange,\n  searchQuery,\n  onWordClick,\n}: EReaderModeProps): React.ReactElement {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const [width, setWidth] = useState<number>(0);\n\n  const paragraphs = useMemo(() => {\n    const map = new Map<number, Token[]>();\n    for (const token of tokens) {\n      const list = map.get(token.paragraphIndex) ?? [];\n      list.push(token);\n      map.set(token.paragraphIndex, list);\n    }\n    return Array.from(map.values());\n  }, [tokens]);\n\n  // Track the container width; columns are sized from it in px (columnWidth takes no %).\n  useLayoutEffect(() => {\n    const el = containerRef.current;\n    if (!el) return;\n    setWidth(el.clientWidth);\n    const observer = new ResizeObserver(() => setWidth(el.clientWidth));\n    observer.observe(el);\n    return () => observer.disconnect();\n  }, []);\n\n  // Count pages whenever the layout can change.\n  useLayoutEffect(() => {\n    const el = containerRef.current;\n    if (!el || width <= 0) return;\n    const count = Math.max(1, Math.round((el.scrollWidth + COLUMN_GAP) / (width + COLUMN_GAP)));\n    onPageCountChange(count);\n  }, [width, paragraphs, prefs.font_family, prefs.font_size, prefs.line_spacing, onPageCountChange]);\n\n  useLayoutEffect(() => {\n    const el = containerRef.current;\n    if (!el || width <= 0) return;\n    el.scrollLeft = page * (width + COLUMN_GAP);\n  }, [page, width, prefs.font_family, prefs.font_size, prefs.line_spacing]);\n\n  // Jump to the page holding the first match when the query changes.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const el = containerRef.current;\n    if (!searchQuery.trim() || !el || width <= 0) return;\n    const first = el.querySelector(\"mark\") as HTMLElement | null;\n    if (first) onPageChange(Math.floor(first.offsetLeft / (width + COLUMN_GAP)));\n  }, [searchQuery, width, onPageChange]);\n\n  const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n    const target = (e.target as HTMLElement).closest(\"[data-word]\");\n    const value = target?.getAttribute(\"data-word\");\n    if (value != null) onWordClick(Number(value));\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex-1 min-h-0 w-full max-w-[46rem] mx-auto px-6 py-4\">\n      <div\n        ref={containerRef}\n        onClick={handleClick}\n        className=\"relative w-full overflow-hidden select-text\"\n        style={{\n          ...READING_TEXT_STYLE,\n          height: \"calc(100vh - 140px)\",\n          columnWidth: width > 0 ? `${width}px` : undefined,\n          columnGap: `${COLUMN_GAP}px`,\n          columnFill: \"auto\",\n        }}\n      >\n        {paragraphs.map((paraTokens, pIdx) => (\n          <p key={pIdx} className=\"mb-6\">\n            {paraTokens.map((token, tIdx) => (\n              <React.Fragment key={token.index}>\n                {tIdx > 0 && \" \"}\n                <span data-word={token.index} className=\"cursor-pointer hover:underline\">\n                  {highlight(token.text, searchQuery)}\n                </span>\n              </React.Fragment>\n            ))}\n          </p>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"}

The reader takes\n * the whole viewport (like the whiteboard); the library, stats and passage\n * manager render inside the workspace shell. {"file_path": "C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/src/features/reading-center/components/WpmDialog.tsx", "content": "import React, { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from \"@/components/ui/dialog\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { WPM_MAX, WPM_MIN } from \"../lib/constants\";\n\nconst clampWpm = (v: number): number => Math.max(WPM_MIN, Math.min(WPM_MAX, v));\n\nexport function WpmDialog({\n  open,\n  onOpenChange,\n  value,\n  onSave,\n}: {\n  open: boolean;\n  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;\n  value: number;\n  onSave: (v: number) => void;\n}): React.ReactElement {\n  const [local, setLocal] = useState<number>(value);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (open) setLocal(value);\n  }, [open, value]);\n\n  const step = (delta: number) => setLocal((v) => clampWpm(v + delta));\n\n  return (\n    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>\n      <DialogContent className=\"max-w-sm\">\n        <DialogHeader>\n          <DialogTitle>Reading Speed (WPM)</DialogTitle>\n        </DialogHeader>\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr_auto] items-center gap-4 py-4\">\n          <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n            <Button variant=\"outline\" onClick={() => step(-10)}>-10</Button>\n            <Button variant=\"outline\" onClick={() => step(-100)}>-100</Button>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"text-center text-5xl font-semibold tabular-nums\" aria-live=\"polite\">{local}</div>\n          <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n            <Button variant=\"outline\" onClick={() => step(10)}>+10</Button>\n            <Button variant=\"outline\" onClick={() => step(100)}>+100</Button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <DialogFooter>\n          <Button variant=\"ghost\" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>Cancel</Button>\n          <Button\n            onClick={() => {\n              onSave(local);\n              onOpenChange(false);\n            }}\n          >\n            Save\n          </Button>\n        </DialogFooter>\n      </DialogContent>\n    </Dialog>\n  );\n}\n"}

 >\n          The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog.\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction SwitchRow({\n  label,\n  help,\n  checked,\n  onChange,\n}: {\n  label: `${remainingText.replace(\"remaining\", \"to go\")} - ${wpm} WPM` : null;\n\n  return (\n    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>\n      <DialogContent className=\"flex h-screen w-screen max-w-none flex-col gap-0 rounded-none p-0 sm:rounded-none\">\n        <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border-b px-6 py-4 pr-14\">\n          <DialogTitle className=\"text-lg\">{title}</DialogTitle>\n          <Badge variant=\"secondary\">Passage</Badge>\n          <DialogDescription className=\"sr-only\">The full passage text.</DialogDescription>\n          <Input\n            value={query}\n            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}\n            placeholder=\"Search this text...\"\n            className=\"ml-auto w-full max-w-xs\"\n          />\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-6 py-8\">\n          <div className=\"mx-auto max-w-[46rem]\" style={readingTextStyleFor(prefs)}>\n            {paragraphs.map((para, pIdx) => (\n              <p key={pIdx} className=\"mb-6\">\n                {para.map((t, i) => {\n                  const match = needle !== \"\" && normalize(t.text).includes(needle);\n                  const current = currentWord === t.index;\n                  const cls = `rounded-sm ${match ? (\n                        <button\n                          type=\"button\"\n                          className={`${cls} cursor-pointer border-0 bg-transparent p-0 text-inherit [font:inherit] hover:underline`}\n                          onClick={() => {\n                            onJump(t.index);\n                            onOpenChange(false);\n                          }}\n                        >\n                          {t.text}\n                        </button>\n                      ) : (\n                        <span className={cls}>{t.text}</span>\n                      )}\n                    </React.Fragment>\n                  );\n                })}\n              </p>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {(onPlay || footer) && (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-4 border-t px-6 py-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n            {onPlay && (\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                aria-label=\"Play\"\n                onClick={() => {\n                  onPlay();\n                  onOpenChange(false);\n                }}\n                className=\"flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary text-primary-foreground\"\n              >\n                <Play className=\"h-5 w-5\" />\n              </button>\n            )}\n            {footer && <span>{footer}</span>}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </DialogContent>\n    </Dialog>\n  );\n}\n"}
